# ADCIRC fort.* files — purpose, I/O, and connectivity

## Scope note

This note maps every standard `fort.NN` filename in an ADCIRC run to:
- **purpose** (what the file holds),
- **I/O direction** (input / output / both),
- **reader/writer subroutine** with `file:line` from the v55 source tree,
- **manual reference** (ADCIRC Wiki / official PDFs) where the format/parameters are documented,
- **dependencies** (which other `fort.*` files or fort.15 controls drive it).

Coverage focuses on the main solver path under `models/adcirc/source_code/adcirc/{src,prep,util,wind}/`. Parallel-only artifacts (per-PE `fort.18`, MPI staging) and clearly deprecated units (e.g., legacy `fort.33` solver log) are noted but not detailed.

## Source basis

- **Source-code map** — built 2026-05-05 by scanning `OPEN/READ/WRITE/CLOSE` statements across the main solver tree via Codex (`gpt-5.3-codex`). Cited as `[file=src/... line=N]`.
- **Manual cross-reference** — retrieved via rag_proxy against the `manuals` collection filtered to `model=adcirc` (15 K chunks: official PDFs, ADCIRC Wiki, workshop materials). Cited as `[file=wiki:adcirc:Fort.NN_file]` or `[file=pdf:adcirc:<stem> page=N]`.

Both source and manual citations are preserved per claim. When the two disagree, the source code is treated as ground truth.

## Why this note exists

Every ADCIRC run reads at least `fort.14` + `fort.15` and writes a subset of `fort.61–93` according to flags inside `fort.15`. The full mapping is fragmented across the User Manual, the Theory document, and dozens of wiki pages. A single reference (with file:line citations into the actual reader) lets future debugging skip the manual scan and go straight to the right subroutine.

Cartesian 좌표를 직접 사용한다. 구면 좌표의 도를 라디안으로 바꾼다. 부족한 레코드는 읽기에서 실패한다. 남는 레코드는 읽지 않는다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/elev_stat151.rst line=6]` `` The reading of the elev_stat.151 file is triggered when the number of elevation recording stations (:ref:`NSTAE`) in the :doc:`fort.15 <fort15>` file is set to a negative value. ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/elev_stat151.rst line=13]` `` :ref:`NSTAE2` ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/elev_stat151.rst line=15]` `` :ref:`XEL(k) <XEL>`, :ref:`YEL(k) <YEL>` ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/elev_stat151.rst line=21]` ` If the value of NSTAE2 differs from the value of NSTAE (as read from the fort.15 file) the value of NSTAE2 will be used. ## File inventory

### Required input files

| fort# | Purpose | Reader | Source citation | Manual reference |
|-------|---------|--------|-----------------|------------------|
| **14** | Mesh geometry: nodes, elements, boundary conditions | `read_global` / `readMesh` | `[file=src/read_input.F line=380]`, `[file=prep/read_global.F line=96]` | `[file=wiki:adcirc:Fort.14_file]` — "contains the grid coordinates, element identifiers and boundary conditions for the model run" |
| **15** | Master control: physics flags (NOLIBF/NOLIFA/NOLICA), forcing selectors (NWS, NTIP), output specifiers (NOUT*) | `READ_INPUT`, `READ_INPUT_3D` | `[file=src/read_input.F line=394]`, `[file=src/read_input.F line=5296]` | `[file=wiki:adcirc:Fort.15_file]`; key parameters at `[file=wiki:adcirc:NOLIBF]`, `[file=wiki:adcirc:NOLIFA]`, `[file=wiki:adcirc:NWS]` |

### Optional input files (selected by `fort.15` flags)

| fort# | Purpose | Activation flag (in fort.15) | Reader | Source citation | Manual reference |
|-------|---------|------------------------------|--------|-----------------|------------------|
| **13** | 노드 속성 (Manning·primitive weighting·교각·canopy 등) | `NWP > 0` | ReadNodalAttr | `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1080]` ` DO k=1,NWP `; `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1081]` ` READ(15,*) AttrName ` | Chezy·Manning·quadratic 마찰 속성은 NOLIBF가 1 또는 3이어야 한다. (`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1850]` ` IF ((LoadChezy.or.LoadManningsN.or.LoadQuadraticFric).and. `; `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1851]` ` &     NoLiBF.ne.1.AND.NOLIBF.NE.3) THEN `; `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1855]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `) 현행 매개변수 문서와 코드의 주석·오류 문장은 1만 적는다. (`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=387]` ` Parameter controlling the type of bottom stress parameterization used in a 2DDI ADCIRC run. This parameter must be specified but is ignored in a 3D run. Note: In the NWP section, if the user selects quadratic_friction_coefficient_at_sea_floor, mannings_n_at_sea_floor, or chezy_friction_coefficient_at_sea_floor, then NOLIBF must be 1 (nonlinear friction formulation) since all those formulations are nonlinear. If the NOLIBF were anything other than 1, it is an error that will cause ADCIRC to stop. `; `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1848]` ` C     ERROR CHECK: If Chezy, Manning's or Quadratic friction was loaded `; `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1849]` ` C     from the nodal attributes file, NOLIBF must be 1. `) |
| **22** | NWS가 선택하는 기상 입력 | 기상 NWS별 조건 | NWS별 reader | `[file=src/read_input.F line=1769]` ` READ(15,FMT=*,END=99998,ERR=99999) NWS ` | NWS=12는 OWI ASCII 입력을 선택한다. (`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=298]` ` **NWS = 12** `; `[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=299]` ` Wind velocity (10 minute averaged winds at 10m) and atmospheric pressure are provided in the OWI format on one or two rectangular (lat/lon) grid(s). If two grids are used, the first is designated as the large ("basin") scale grid, and the second is designated as the small ("region") scale grid. The Single File Meteorological Forcing Input File (fort.22) is only used to specify a few configuration parameters, while the actual wind fields are recorded in files named: `) NWS=13은 OWI NetCDF 입력을 선택한다. (`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws13.rst line=10]` ` Oceanweather Inc. (OWI) NetCDF Meteorological Input Files `; `[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws13.rst line=214]` ` -  *NWS13File*: optional file name for netCDF file ( *fort.22.nc* default ) `) NCICE=12와 양수 기상 NWS=1·2·7의 조합은 호환성 검사에서 종료한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=2322]` ` IF((NCICE.GT.0).AND. `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=2323]` ` &       ((NWS.EQ.1).OR.(NWS.EQ.2).OR.(NWS.EQ.7))) THEN `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=2328]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `) |
| **23** | 파랑 방사 응력 입력 | NRS=1 또는 2 | NRS=1: rsinit / rsget. NRS=2: RS2INIT | `[file=src/cstart.F line=312]` ` IF(NRS.EQ.1) THEN `; `[file=src/cstart.F line=313]` ` OPEN(23,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.23') `; `[file=src/cstart.F line=317]` ` IF(NRS.EQ.2) THEN `; `[file=src/cstart.F line=318]` ` CALL RS2INIT(RSNX1,RSNY1,NP) ` | NRS=1은 노드별 응력을 읽는다. (`[file=src/wind.F line=7549]` ` read(pbljagf,'(i8,5e13.5)',end=500,err=600,iostat=errorio) `; `[file=src/wind.F line=7550]` ` &         nhg, rsnxRaw, rsnyRaw `) NRS=2는 fort.23을 STWAVE 제어 파일로 읽는다. (`[file=src/rs2.F line=89]` ` OPEN(23,FILE=TRIM(GBLINPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.23',STATUS='OLD') `; `[file=src/rs2.F line=93]` ` read(23,*,err=99999) type `; `[file=src/rs2.F line=438]` ` read(23,*,err=99999) ns    !number of stwave grid files `) 이 초기화는 NRS=3·4·5에서 fort.23을 읽지 않는다. (`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort23.rst line=6]` `` The fort.23 file contains wave radiation stress data that can be used to drive ADCIRC either by itself or in combination with other forcing (including winds). This file is read when ABS(:ref:`NWS <NWS>`) >= 100 in the :ref:`Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File <fort15>`. The format is similar to the meteorological input file used when :ref:`NWS <NWS>` = -4 (i.e. the PBL hurricane model input format following a hot start). ``<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=312]` ` IF(NRS.EQ.1) THEN `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=313]` ` OPEN(23,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.23') `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=317]` ` IF(NRS.EQ.2) THEN `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=318]` ` CALL RS2INIT(RSNX1,RSNY1,NP) `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=1466]` ` SELECT CASE(NRS) `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=1467]` ` CASE(1) `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=1471]` ` CASE(2) `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=1476]` ` CASE(3,4,5) `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=1477]` ` ! do nothing, initialization takes place in timestep.F `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=2271]` ` IF(NRS.EQ.3) THEN `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=2274]` ` &    /,9X,'WAVES WILL BE COUPLED TO SWAN!') `) |
| 23 / NRS=1·STWAVE type=2 | 노드 응력·제어·경계·방사 응력 자료 레코드 | NRS=1 또는 NRS=2, type=2 | RS2INIT / rs2get_type2 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort23.rst line=15]` `` :ref:`JN <JN>`, :ref:`RSX(JN) <RSX>`, :ref:`RSY(JN) <RSY>` ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort23.rst line=22]` `` 2. Radiation stresses are input directly to a subset of nodes in the ADCIRC grid (as specified by the node number :ref:`JN <JN>`). ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort23.rst line=26]` ` 4. Each data line must have the format I8, 2E13.5. Data input lines are repeated for as many nodes as desired. A line containing the # symbol in column 2 indicates radiation stress data at the next time increment begins on the following line. At each new time, any node that is not specified in the input file is assumed to have zero wave radiation stress. `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7535]` ` rsnx(1:np) = 0.d0 `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7536]` ` rsny(1:np) = 0.d0 `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7540]` ` read(23,'(a80)',end=500,err=600,iostat=errorio) pbljagf `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7541]` ` if (pbljagf(1:1).eq.'#') then `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7542]` ` call terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7545]` ` if(pbljagf(2:2).ne.'#') exit `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7549]` ` read(pbljagf,'(i8,5e13.5)',end=500,err=600,iostat=errorio) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7550]` ` &         nhg, rsnxRaw, rsnyRaw `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7551]` ` rsnx(nhg) = rsnxRaw `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7552]` ` rsny(nhg) = rsnyRaw `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7555]` ` if (pbljagf(1:1).eq.'#') then `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7556]` ` call terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7560]` ` if(pbljagf(2:2).eq.'#') exit `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort23.rst line=28]` ` 5. Wave radiation stress must be input in units of velocity squared (consistent with the units of gravity). Stress in these units is obtained by dividing stress in units of force/area by the reference density of water. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2112]` ` Applied wave radiation stress in the x,y directions divided by the reference density of water (should be units (length/time)2). An oceanographic convention is used where stress is positive when it is pointed in positive coordinate directions. `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7551]` ` rsnx(nhg) = rsnxRaw `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7552]` ` rsny(nhg) = rsnyRaw `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort23.rst line=20]` ` 1. At least two datasets must be present in the file to allow for time interpolation. If only one dataset is present, the run will terminate with an unexpected end-of-file error. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort23.rst line=24]` `` 3. If ADCIRC is cold started, the first set of radiation stress data corresponds to TIME=:ref:`STATIM <STATIM>`. If ADCIRC is hot started, the first set of radiation stress data corresponds to TIME=HOT START TIME. Additional sets of radiation stress data must be provided every :ref:`RSTIMINC <RSTIMINC>`, where :ref:`RSTIMINC <RSTIMINC>` is the radiation stress time interval and is specified in the :ref:`Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File <fort15>`. Radiation stresses are interpolated in time to the ADCIRC time step. ``<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=310]` ` RSTIME1 = STATIM*86400.D0 `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=311]` ` RSTIME2 = RSTIME1+RSTIMINC `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=314]` ` CALL RSGET(RSNX1,RSNY1) `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=315]` ` CALL RSGET(RSNX2,RSNY2) `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=1464]` ` RSTIME1 = TimeLoc `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=1465]` ` RSTIME2 = RSTIME1 + RSTIMINC `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=1469]` ` CALL RSGET(RSNX1,RSNY1) `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=1470]` ` CALL RSGET(RSNX2,RSNY2) `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort23.rst line=30]` ` 6. Data must be provided for the entire model run, otherwise the run will crash! `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7566]` ` 500   call allMessage(ERROR,'Unexpectedly reached end-of-file.') ! | **25 / 225 / 227** | OWI 얼음 농도 입력 | NCICE=12 | NCICE1_INIT | `[file=src/owi_ice.F line=132]` ` OPEN(225,FILE=TRIM(GBLINPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.225', STATUS='OLD') `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=158]` ` OPEN(227,FILE=TRIM(GBLINPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.227',STATUS='OLD') ` | 현행 RST 제목의 fort.255·277은 본문과 코드의 fort.225·227과 다르다. (`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort25.rst line=3]` ` Fort.25, 255/277: Ice Coverage Input Files `; `[file=src/owi_ice.F line=132]` ` OPEN(225,FILE=TRIM(GBLINPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.225', STATUS='OLD') `; `[file=src/owi_ice.F line=158]` ` OPEN(227,FILE=TRIM(GBLINPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.227',STATUS='OLD') `) `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort25.rst line=8]` ` In order to use ice field data, two changes are required to an existing fort.15: the ten thousands place must be set to 12 (see the NWS Simplified Table of Values for details), and the time increment of the ice field data (CICE_TIMINC) in seconds must be supplied on the WTIMINC line (see the Supplemental Meteorology/Wave/Ice Parameters table for details). Ice coverage datasets are linearly interpolated in time by ADCIRC, as a result, at least two datasets are required. `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1775]` ` NCICE = INT(ABS(NWS)/1000) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1776]` ` NWS = INT(ABS(NWS)-NCICE*1000)*INT(NWS/ABS(NWS))  !RESETTING NWS/NRS `<br>문서는 -1.0%를 0.0%로 처리한다고 적는다. 코드는 보간 셀의 음수 모서리 값을 비음수 모서리 평균으로 바꾼다. 모든 모서리가 음수일 때만 평균의 초기값 0이 남는다. `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort25.rst line=21]` ` If numIceFields in the fort.25 file is set to 1, only the fort.225 file will be read; if numIceFields in the fort.25 file is set to 2 then both the fort.225 and fort.227 files will be read. The fort.225 (the basin scale, i.e., large scale coarse grid) and optional fort.227 (the region scale, i.e., smaller scale finer grid) files contain the actual ice percentage values (0.0 to 100.0%) and a value of -1.0 which means land or no data available. A value of -1.0% is treated as 0.0% ice in ADCIRC. `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=367]` ` CV(1) = CiceB(xi,yi) `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=368]` ` CV(2) = CiceB(xi+1,yi) `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=369]` ` CV(3) = CiceB(xi+1,yi+1) `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=370]` ` CV(4) = CiceB(xi,yi+1) `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=374]` ` if(CV(k) .ge. 0.d0) then `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=375]` ` CVbar = CVbar + CV(k) `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=376]` ` ibar = ibar + 1 `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=379]` ` if (ibar.gt.0) CVbar = CVbar/real(ibar,8) `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=381]` ` if (CV(k).lt.0.d0) CV(k) = CVbar `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=385]` ` Cice1=w1*CV(1)+w2*CV(2)+w3*CV(3)+w4*CV(4) ` |

|-------|---------|------|--------|-----------------|------------------|

### Output files — meteorological

농도 전역 출력 NOUTGC의 파일명은 fort.83이다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=1213]` ` ConcDescript % lun                  = 83 `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1214]` ` ConcDescript % specifier            = NOUTGC `)
fort.77 헤더의 자료 세트 수는 ndsets_tvw이다. (`[file=src/globalio.F line=1188]` ` write(lun, 1010) ndsets_tvw, size_this, dtdp*nspool_tvw, `; `[file=src/globalio.F line=1189]` ` &                 nspool_tvw, 1, FileFmtVersion `)
fort.72의 실제 보간 대상은 WVNXOUT·WVNYOUT이다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=807]` ` WindVelStaDescript % interped_array       => wvnxout `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=808]` ` WindVelStaDescript % interped_array2      => wvnyout `)
fort.83의 ASCII 값은 CH1이다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=1219]` ` ConcDescript % array                => CH1 `)

## Connectivity graph

```
                    ┌────────────────────┐
                    │  fort.15 (control) │
                    │  - NWS, NWP, NTIP  │
                    │  - NOUT* flags     │
                    │  - harmonic setup  │
                    └────────┬───────────┘
                             │ drives
        ┌────────────┬───────┴────────┬──────────────┐
        ▼            ▼                ▼              ▼
   fort.14       fort.13         fort.22        fort.23/24/25
   (mesh)        (nodal attr)    (met forcing)  (waves / SAL / ice)
        │            │                │              │
        └────────────┴────────────────┴──────────────┘
                     │
                     ▼
              ADCIRC main loop
                     │
        ┌────────────┼────────────┬──────────────┬───────────────┐
        ▼            ▼            ▼              ▼               ▼
   fort.61–65   fort.71–74   fort.41–42    fort.51–53      fort.81/83/90/92
   (2D out)     (met out)    (3D station) (harmonic)     (concentration/tau0/sponge)
                     │
                     ▼
              fort.80 metadata
              merges per-PE outputs
              after parallel run
```

## Decision Guide

- 일반 수위·유속·농도 출력은 NOUT 계열을 descriptor에 연결한다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=512]` ` ElevStaDescript % specifier            = NOUTE `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=548]` ` VelStaDescript % specifier            = NOUTV `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1214]` ` ConcDescript % specifier            = NOUTGC `)
- NHSTAR는 핫스타트 출력 형식을 선택한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=4522]` ` READ(15,*) NHSTAR,NHSINC `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4535]` ` SELECT CASE(NHSTAR) `)
- NHSINC는 핫스타트 출력 간격을 정한다. (`[file=src/timestep.F line=1196]` ` ITEST=(IT/NHSINC)*NHSINC `; `[file=src/timestep.F line=1200]` ` if ((ABS(NHSTAR) > 0 .and. ITEST == IT) .or. (-IHOT == IT)) then  !tcm v51.26 added abs(nhstar) to handle when nhstar = -1 `)
- v56.2.1은 잘못된 NHSTAR를 거부한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=4553]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, message="Input value of NHSTAR is invalid.") `)
- 코드는 `(NHSINC.EQ.0).AND.(NHSTAR.NE.0)` 조건에서 종료한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=4556]` ` IF((NHSINC.EQ.0).AND.(NHSTAR.NE.0)) THEN `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4557]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `)
- 두 실행의 NHSTAR/NHSINC 변경이 일반 출력 형식의 조용한 표류를 일으키는지는 확인하지 않음. <!-- source-needed: 실행 결과 근거 없음 -->
- 출력 제어값의 절댓값은 형식을 선택한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=3627]` ` SELECT CASE(ABS(NOUTE)) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=3632]` ` CASE(1) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=3637]` ` CASE(3) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=3642]` ` CASE(5) `)
- v56.2.1은 NOUTE의 절댓값 2를 거부한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=3634]` ` CASE(2) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=3635]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=3636]` ` &                     message='Binary format output is no longer supported.') `)
- 핫스타트 후 음수 제어값은 새 관측소 출력 파일을 시작한다. (`[file=src/hstart.F line=1562]` ` IF(NOUTE.LT.0) THEN `; `[file=src/hstart.F line=1563]` ` NSCOUE=0 `; `[file=src/hstart.F line=1571]` ` 1041   FORMAT(//,' A NEW ELEVATION STATION FILE WILL BE STARTED') `)
- 핫스타트 후 양수 제어값은 기존 출력 파일에 이어 쓴다. (`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=858]` ` NOUTE = 1 Output is provided at the selected elevation recording stations in standard ascii format. Following a hot start, continued output is merged into the existing fort.61 file. `)
- NWS=12는 OWI ASCII 입력을 선택한다. (`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=298]` ` **NWS = 12** `; `[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=299]` ` Wind velocity (10 minute averaged winds at 10m) and atmospheric pressure are provided in the OWI format on one or two rectangular (lat/lon) grid(s). If two grids are used, the first is designated as the large ("basin") scale grid, and the second is designated as the small ("region") scale grid. The Single File Meteorological Forcing Input File (fort.22) is only used to specify a few configuration parameters, while the actual wind fields are recorded in files named: `)
- NWS=13은 OWI NetCDF 입력을 선택한다. (`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws13.rst line=10]` ` Oceanweather Inc. (OWI) NetCDF Meteorological Input Files `; `[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws13.rst line=214]` ` -  *NWS13File*: optional file name for netCDF file ( *fort.22.nc* default ) `)
- cold start와 hot start의 NWS 변경으로 hot restart가 손상되는지는 확인하지 않음. <!-- source-needed: 실행 결과 근거 없음 -->
- Chezy·Manning·quadratic 마찰 속성은 NOLIBF가 1 또는 3이어야 한다. (`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1850]` ` IF ((LoadChezy.or.LoadManningsN.or.LoadQuadraticFric).and. `; `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1851]` ` &     NoLiBF.ne.1.AND.NOLIBF.NE.3) THEN `; `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1855]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `)
- 코드의 주석과 오류 문장은 NOLIBF=1만 적는다. (`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1848]` ` C     ERROR CHECK: If Chezy, Manning's or Quadratic friction was loaded `; `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1849]` ` C     from the nodal attributes file, NOLIBF must be 1. `)

## Working Rules

1. Treat `fort.14` + `fort.15` as the canonical pair — never touch the mesh without keeping the matching `fort.15` (or you get mismatched `NSTAE`/`NSTAV` arrays).
2. Always inspect `fort.80` after a parallel run before opening `fort.61/62/...` — those files are split per PE before merge.
3. 2차원 ASCII 시계열의 첫 줄은 RUNDES,RUNID,AGRID이다. (`[file=src/globalio.F line=971]` ` write(lun, 1000) rundes, runid, agrid `) 조화분석 ASCII는 NFREQ로 시작한다. (`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort51.rst line=13]` `` :ref:`NFREQ <NFREQ>` ``; `[file=src/write_output.F line=2765]` ` write(lun,*) nfreq+nf `) binary 핫스타트는 FileFmtVersion으로 시작한다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=5012]` ` WRITE(hss % lun,REC=IHOTSTP) FileFmtVersion `) netCDF는 해당 포맷과 변수 스키마로 판독한다. (`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=3945]` ` case (3, 367, 368) `; `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=3946]` ` myFile%ncformat = NF90_CLOBBER `)
4. POST51은 첫 지역 파일의 NFREQ로 다른 지역 파일을 읽는다. (`[file=prep/post.F line=1315]` ` READ(NFREQMSG,*) NFREQ `; `[file=prep/post.F line=1326]` ` DO I=1, NFREQ `; `[file=prep/post.F line=1327]` ` READ(LOC2(IPROC),'(A80)') INLINE `) 다른 지역 파일의 주파수 수는 IDUM으로 읽는다. (`[file=prep/post.F line=1325]` ` READ(INLINE,*) IDUM `) 이 구간은 주파수 수의 동일성을 검사하지 않는다. (`[file=prep/post.F line=1325]` ` READ(INLINE,*) IDUM `; `[file=prep/post.F line=1326]` ` DO I=1, NFREQ `; `[file=prep/post.F line=1327]` ` READ(LOC2(IPROC),'(A80)') INLINE `) 조용히 주파수를 버리는 결과는 확인하지 않음. <!-- source-needed: 실행 결과 근거 없음 -->

## Common Pitfalls

- 형식은 출력 제어값의 절댓값으로 확인한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=3627]` ` SELECT CASE(ABS(NOUTE)) `) 핫스타트 뒤 기존 파일 처리 방식은 부호로 확인한다. (`[file=src/hstart.F line=1562]` ` IF(NOUTE.LT.0) THEN `; `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=3888]` ` if ((IHOT == 0) .or. (descript%specifier < 0) .or. & `; `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=3889]` ` (myFile%fileFound .eqv. .false.)) then `) 부호 변경이 부분 파일을 손상시키는지는 확인하지 않음. <!-- source-needed: 실행 결과 근거 없음 -->
- **fort.13 silent ignore** — if `NWP=0` in fort.15, the file is never read no matter what you put in it. Check `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1051]` for `NWP` parsing.
- NWS=13의 reader는 owiwind_netcdf.F의 OWI NetCDF 경로이다. (`[file=src/owiwind_netcdf.F line=417]` ` SUBROUTINE NWS13GET(TimeLoc,WVNX2,WVNY2,PRN2, `) NWS=14의 reader는 wind.F의 NWS14INIT·NWS14GET이다. (`[file=src/wind.F line=2002]` ` CALL NWS14INIT(NWS) `; `[file=src/wind.F line=8394]` ` SUBROUTINE NWS14GET(WVNX2,WVNY2,PRN2,CICE2) `)

| 항목 | 문서·코드 대조 | 출처와 원문 |
|---|---|---|
| NC13-02 NWS=±13의 입력 종류 설명 | NWS 종합 페이지는 NWS=5 형식의 fort.22와 WRAMP를 설명한다. 전용 nws13.rst와 코드는 OWI netCDF 그룹 입력을 사용한다. 코드 판은 v56.2.1이다. 이 불일치는 현행 rst 사이에도 있다. | `[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=93]` ` * - 13, -13 `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=94]` ` - Ramped meteorological forcing `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=95]` ` - Similar to NWS=5/-5, but applies a ramping function to the meteorological inputs over a user-specified time period `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=96]` ` - fort.22 `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=308]` ` **NWS = 13, -13** `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=309]` ` Similar to NWS = 5/-5, but applies a ramping function to the meteorological inputs. Wind velocity and atmospheric pressure are read in at all grid nodes from the Single File Meteorological Forcing Input File. The positive/negative convention for time reference is the same as with NWS = 5/-5. The meteorological forcing is ramped up over a user-specified time period using the WRAMP parameter. `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=2041]` ` WTIME1=STATIM*86400.D0 `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=2045]` ` CALL NWS13INIT(0.D0,W) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=2046]` ` CALL NWS13GET(WTIME1,WVNX1,WVNY1,PRN1, `<br>`[file=src/owiwind_netcdf.F line=262]` ` call check_err(NF90_OPEN(TRIM(ADJUSTL(NWS13File)),NF90_NOWRITE,NC_ID)) `<br>`[file=src/owiwind_netcdf.F line=265]` ` call check_err(NF90_GET_ATT(NC_ID,NF90_GLOBAL,"group_order",GroupOrder)) ` |
| NC13-08 time: 날짜 문자열 접두사 | 설명은 minutes from을 적는다. 코드는 날짜 앞 14자를 건너뛴다. 예제의 minutes since 접두사는 14자이다. 설명의 minutes from 접두사는 같은 위치에 날짜를 놓지 않는다. | `[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws13.rst line=97]` ` -  *time* (time), Datetime-number with units of “minutes from YYYY-mm-dd `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws13.rst line=98]` ` HH:MM:SS” `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws13.rst line=129]` ` \|              time:units = "minutes since 1990-01-01T01:00:00" ; `<br>`[file=src/owiwind_netcdf.F line=319]` ` call check_err(NF90_INQ_VARID(NC_IDG,"time",NC_VAR)) `<br>`[file=src/owiwind_netcdf.F line=320]` ` call check_err(NF90_GET_ATT(NC_IDG,NC_VAR,"units",TimeUnits)) `<br>`[file=src/owiwind_netcdf.F line=321]` ` READ(UNIT=TimeUnits,FMT='(14X,I4,1X,I2,1X,I2,1X,I2,1X,I2,1X,I2)') `<br>`[file=src/owiwind_netcdf.F line=322]` ` &    Year,Month,Day,Hour,Minute,Second ` |
| NC13-12 -I 경로 예외 | 코드는 NWS13File을 그대로 연다. 코드는 INPUTDIR·GBLINPUTDIR를 붙이지 않는다. 지정 문자열이 절대 경로인지 상대 경로인지에 따라 위치가 달라진다. | `[file=docs/user_guide/running_adcirc/command_line_options.rst line=11]` ``` ``-I INPUTDIR`` ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/running_adcirc/command_line_options.rst line=12]` ` Look for input files in the directory 'INPUTDIR' instead of looking in the directory where the ADCIRC command was issued. `<br>`[file=src/owiwind_netcdf.F line=262]` ` call check_err(NF90_OPEN(TRIM(ADJUSTL(NWS13File)),NF90_NOWRITE,NC_ID)) ` |

### fort.22 NWS별 입력 형식 대조 (v56.2.1)

| 항목 | 문서·코드 대조 | 출처와 원문 |
|---|---|---|
| MET-02 NWS=2: WTIMINC 레코드 | rst 형식은 fort.22에 WTIMINC 레코드를 적는다. 문서의 설명 문장은 fort.15에 WTIMINC를 지정하도록 적는다. 코드는 fort.22에서 WTIMINC를 읽지 않는다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=119]` `` :ref:`WTIMINC <WTIMINC>` ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=124]` ` Repeat the block above for each time step until the end of the simulation. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=133]` ` * Additional sets of met. data must be provided every WTIMINC, where WTIMINC is the met. data time interval and is specified in the Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File. `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=1816]` ` OPEN(22,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.22') `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=1817]` ` READ(22,*) (NHG,WVNX1(I),WVNY1(I),PRN1(I),I=1,NP) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=1818]` ` READ(22,*) (NHG,WVNX2(I),WVNY2(I),PRN2(I),I=1,NP) ` |
| MET-05 NWS=3의 좌표계 의무 | 문서는 ADCIRC 격자가 lon·lat이어야 한다고 적는다. 코드는 ICS=1에서 Cartesian X·Y를 사용한 별도 보간 경로도 갖는다. 이 차이는 입력 허용 경로의 차이다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=170]` ` * Meteorological data is input to a longitude, latitude grid and interpolated in space onto the ADCIRC grid. The ADCIRC grid must be in lon, lat coordinates. `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3326]` ` IF(ICS.NE.1) THEN `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3327]` ` YCOOR=SFEA(I)*RAD2DEG `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3328]` ` XCOOR=SLAM(I)*RAD2DEG `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3330]` ` IF(ICS.EQ.1) THEN `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3331]` ` YCOOR=Y(I) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3332]` ` XCOOR=X(I) ` |

PRN2(NHG)=101300.d0/RHOWATG                       !RAL0315+ OK ` |

## 문서와 코드(v56.2.1) 입력이 다른 곳

문서 판과 코드 판이 달라 생기는 차이가 섞여 있다. 문서에는 현행 RST·위키·웹사이트 v53·PDF v44.XX·1990년대 보고서가 포함된다. 코드는 v56.2.1이다. 아래 행은 두 입력 대조 보고서 §1이 가리키는 판정을 따른다. PDF를 직접 근거로 사용한 입력 판정은 이 표에 없다. ([fort.15 대조 보고서 §1](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-fort15-vs-code.md#1-요약); [입력 파일 대조 보고서 §1](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-inputfiles-vs-code.md#1-요약))

| 파일·변수 | 문서 위치와 원문 | 코드 src/파일:줄과 원문 | 차이 |
|---|---|---|---|
| fort.15 / ` IM ` | 2D/3D·점성·이류·GWCE 선택. 전문 문서는 0,1,2,10,11,20,21,30,31과 여섯 자리 선택을 적는다.<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst line=38]` ` * - 0 `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst line=41]` ` * - 1 `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst line=44]` ` * - 2 `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst line=47]` ` * - 10 `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst line=50]` ` * - 11 `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst line=53]` ` * - 20 `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst line=56]` ` * - 21 `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst line=59]` ` * - 30 `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst line=62]` ` * - 31 `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst line=95]` ` * - 1 (default) `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort15.rst line=22]` `` :ref:`IM <IM>` `` | 필수 정수를 읽는다. 20/30은 폐기 선택으로 종료한다. 여섯 자리 수는 숫자 범위로 받는다. 자리별 분기는 아래에 적었다.<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1149]` ` READ(15,*) IM `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1184]` ` ELSEIF (IM.EQ.20.or.IM.eq.30) THEN `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1185]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1186]` ` &                  message="IM = 20 and 30 are deprecated. "// `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1187]` ` &                          "Use the densityControl namelist to "// `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1188]` ` &                          "set baroclinicity for 2DDI simulations "// `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1189]` ` &                          "(see adcirc wiki for more details).") `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1233]` ` ELSEIF ((IM.GE.111111).AND.(IM.LE.734323)) THEN `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1377]` ` ELSE `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1378]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, ` | 문서의 20/30과 현재 종료 처리가 다르다. 자리별 문서도 첫 자리 7과 마지막 자리의 추가 선택을 빠뜨린다. |
| fort.15 / ` NOLIBF ` | 2DDI 마찰 0=선형,1=제곱,2=혼합. 3D에서는 무시. Chezy/Manning/제곱 마찰 속성에는 반드시 1이며 다른 값이면 종료한다고 적는다.<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=387]` ` Parameter controlling the type of bottom stress parameterization used in a 2DDI ADCIRC run. This parameter must be specified but is ignored in a 3D run. Note: In the NWP section, if the user selects quadratic_friction_coefficient_at_sea_floor, mannings_n_at_sea_floor, or chezy_friction_coefficient_at_sea_floor, then NOLIBF must be 1 (nonlinear friction formulation) since all those formulations are nonlinear. If the NOLIBF were anything other than 1, it is an error that will cause ADCIRC to stop. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=389]` ` = 0 linear bottom friction law. The friction coefficient (FFACTOR) is specified below. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=391]` ` = 1 quadratic bottom friction law. The friction coefficient (FFACTOR) is specified below. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=393]` ` = 2 hybrid nonlinear bottom friction law. In deep water, the friction coefficient is constant and a quadratic bottom friction law results. In shallow water the friction coefficient increases as the depth decreases (e.g. as in a Manning-type friction law). The friction coefficient is determined as: FFACTOR=FFACTORMIN*[1+(HBREAK/H)**FTHETA]**(FGAMMA/FTHETA). The required parameters (FFACTORMIN, HBREAK, FTHETA, FGAMMA) are specified below. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=395]` ` For 3D ADCIRC runs, spatially varying bottom friction should be specified using the bottom_roughness_length nodal attribute. `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolibf.rst line=47]` `` In the :ref:`NWP <nwp>` section, if the user selects ``<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolibf.rst line=48]` ` quadratic_friction_coefficient_at_sea_floor, mannings_n_at_sea_floor, or `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolibf.rst line=49]` ` chezy_friction_coefficient_at_sea_floor, then NOLIBF must be 1 (nonlinear `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolibf.rst line=50]` ` friction formulation) since all those formulations are nonlinear. If the NOLIBF `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolibf.rst line=51]` ` were anything other than 1, it is an error that will cause ADCIRC to stop. ` | 0..3을 허용한다. 3은 혼합층/전체 수심 비에 따른 마찰이다. 해당 절점 속성은 1 또는 3과 조합할 수 있다.<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1484]` ` READ(15,*) NOLIBF `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1485]` ` IF((NOLIBF.LT.0).OR.(NOLIBF.GT.3)) THEN `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1498]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1503]` ` IF (NOLIBF.EQ.0) WRITE(16,2050) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1505]` ` IF (NOLIBF.EQ.1) WRITE(16,2051) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1507]` ` IF (NOLIBF.EQ.2) WRITE(16,2052) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1512]` ` IF (NOLIBF.EQ.3) WRITE(16,2060) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1513]` ` 2060 FORMAT(9X,'THE MODEL WILL USE STANDARD QUADRATIC BOTTOM FRICTION', `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1514]` ` &     ' WITH A FRICTION FACTOR AS A FUCTION OF MIXED-LAYER DEPTH ', `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1515]` ` &     'TO TOTAL DEPTH RATIO') `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=3168]` ` IF(NOLIBF.EQ.3) READ(15,*) CF,HBREAK,FTHETA `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1850]` ` IF ((LoadChezy.or.LoadManningsN.or.LoadQuadraticFric).and. `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1851]` ` &     NoLiBF.ne.1.AND.NOLIBF.NE.3) THEN `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1855]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, ` | 선택 3과 그 읽기 줄이 없다. NOLIBF=3의 절점 속성도 검사를 통과한다. 3D에서도 입력 범위는 검사한다. |
| fort.15 / ` CORI ` | 공간 일정 Coriolis 계수.1/s. 정의는 항상 읽고 NCOR=0에서만 사용한다고 적는다. RST 형식은 NCOR=1을 조건으로 적는다.<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=754]` ` Constant Coriolis coefficient. This value is always read in, however it is only used in the computations when NCOR = 0. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort15.rst line=61]` `` :ref:`CORI <CORI>`      if NCOR = 1 ``<br>`[file=models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/wiki/markdown/Fort.15_file_format.md line=81]` `` `[CORI](/index.php?title=CORI&action=edit&redlink=1)` - constant Coriolis coefficient, only used if `NCOR=0` ``<br>`[file=models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/website_markdown/home/documentation/users-manual-v53/input-file-descriptions/model-parameter-and-periodic-boundary-condition-file-fort-15.md line=502]` ` [**CORI**](https://adcirc.org/home/documentation/users-manual-v53/parameter-definitions#CORI) ` | 항상 읽는다. NCOR=0이면 CORIF=CORI.1이면 위도로 계산한다.<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=3315]` ` READ(15,*) CORI `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=3317]` ` DO I=1,NP `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=3318]` ` CORIF(I)=CORI `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=3326]` ` CORIF(I)=2.0d0*omega*SIN(SFEA(I)) ` | RST 형식의 조건이 반대이다. 정의·위키·v53 형식은 일치한다. v53 문서와 v56.2.1 코드의 판 차이만으로 설명되지 않는 현행 RST 오류이다. |
| fort.15 / ` TAU0 ` | GWCE 가중과 음수 옵션. 양수 상수, -1/-2/-3/-5/-6/-7, 소수 첫 자리 1의 출력을 설명한다. 인용한 정의 값표는 단위를 명시하지 않는다.<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=497]` ` Generalized Wave-Continuity Equation (GWCE) weighting factor that weights the relative contribution of the primitive and wave portions of the GWCE. If "primitive_weighting_in_continuity_equation" is specified as a nodal attribute in the fort.15 file above, this line will be read in but ignored. If a nodal attribute file is not used or "primitive_weighting_in_continuity_equation" is in the nodal attribute (fort.13) file, but not specified in the fort.15 file this TAU0 parameter will be used. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=499]` ` = 0 the GWCE is a pure wave equation. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=501]` ` < 1 the GWCE behaves like a pure primitive continuity equation. A good rule of thumb for setting TAU0 is to set it equal to the largest value of an equivalent linear friction factor (e.g, for linear friction TAU0 = TAU; for quadratic friction TAU0 = maximum (speed*CF/depth). Typical values for TAU0 are in the range of 0.005 – 0.1. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=503]` ` = -1 the TAU0 is spatially varying but constant in time; it is calculated according to depth as follows: If the depth is >=10 TAU0 is set to 0.005, if the depth is < 10, TAU0 is set to 0.020. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=505]` ` = -2 the TAU0 is spatially varying but constant in time; it is calculated according to depth as follows: if the depth is >=200 TAU0 is set to 0.005, if the depth is < 200 but > 1, then TAU0 is set to 1/depth, and if depth < 1, TAU0 is set to 1.0. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=507]` ` = -3 the TAU0 varies spatially and in time. TAU0 is computed from TAU0Base read in from nodal attribute file. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=509]` ` if TAU0Base < 0.025; TAU0 = TAU0Base (constant in time) `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=511]` ` if TAU0Base >= 0.025; TAU0 = TAU0Base + 1.5 TK(i) where TK(i)=Cd\\|U\\|/H `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=521]` ` = -5, FullDomainTimeVaryingTau0 = .True. the TAU0 varies spatially and in time, and is dependent on the local friction; it is limited to a range specified by Tau0FullDomainMin and Tau0FullDomainMax. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=525]` ` For tau0 formulations that vary spatially and temporally, ADCIRC is capable of writing out the tau0 values that it calculates internally. These values are written to a fort.90 file, which has the same format and output frequency as the water surface elevation output file (fort.63). The production of a fort.90 file is specified by placing a 1 in the tenths place of the tau0 input valuie in the fort.15 file. For example, if tau0=-3.1, the calculation of tau0 is still carried out according to the description of tau0=-3 above, and the fort.90 output file will also be produced. `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=169]` ``` -  ``TAU0 = -3, -6, or -7``, :math:`\tau_0` varies in time and based on ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=182]` `` A positive value is ignored if the :ref:`primitive weighting in continuity ``<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=188]` `` GWCE is a pure wave equation for :math:`\tau_0=0`, and approaches a pure ``<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=189]` `` primitive equation as :math:`\tau_0\rightarrow\infty`, however in practice, it ``<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=190]` `` behaves like a pure primitive equation once :math:`\tau_0` reaches 1 or so. ``<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=204]` ``` -  ``If depth >= 10; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= 0.005`` ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=205]` ``` -  ``If depth < 10; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= 0.020`` ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=212]` ``` -  ``If depth >= 200; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= 0.005`` ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=213]` ``` -  ``If 1 < depth < 200; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= 1/depth`` ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=214]` ``` -  ``If depth < 1; then``\ :math:`\tau_0 = 1` ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=227]` ``` -  ``If TAU0Base < 0.025; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= TAU0Base`` (constant in ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=229]` ``` -  ``If TAU0Base >= 0.025; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= TAU0Base+1.5*TK`` ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=245]` ``` -  ``TAU0 = -6``, :math:`\tau_0` is defined using the rules for ``TAU0 = -3``, ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=248]` ``` -  ``TAU0 = -7``, :math:`\tau_0` is defined using the rules for ``TAU0 = -3``, ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=252]` ``` -  :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= AlphaTau0*TAU0VAR + (1.0-AlphaTau0)*LastTau0``, where ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=254]` ``` ``AlphaTau0 = 0.25`` is hard-coded into the model. This means that ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=313]` ``` output is activated by placing a 1 in the tenths place of ``TAU0`` in the ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=314]` ``` fort.15 file. For example, if ``TAU0 = -3.1``, the calculation of :math:`\tau_0` ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=315]` ``` is still carried out according to the description of ``TAU0 = -3`` above, and ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=316]` ` the fort.90 output file will also be produced. ` | 항상 읽는다. -5 계열은 min/max 줄을 읽는다. 절점 속성 초기화가 음수 자리·시간 변화·출력·필수 속성을 처리한다. 초기화 로그는 단위를 1/sec로 기록한다.<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=2500]` ` READ(15,*) Tau0 `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=2505]` ` IF ( (Tau0.le.-5.d0).and.(Tau0.gt.-6.d0) ) THEN `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=2506]` ` READ(15,*) Tau0FullDomainMin, Tau0FullDomainMax `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1829]` ` IF (Tau0.lt.0) THEN `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1832]` ` Tau0Dig2 = INT( (Tau0 - REAL(Tau0Dig1))*10.0d0 - 0.5d0) `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1863]` ` IF ( ((Tau0Dig1.eq.-3).or.(Tau0Dig1.eq.-6)) `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1864]` ` &          .and.(.not.LoadTau0) ) THEN `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1868]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1909]` ` IF (.not.LoadTau0) THEN `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1966]` ` IF ( (Tau0Dig1.eq.-3).or.(Tau0Dig1.eq.-6) `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1977]` ` IF ( Tau0Dig1.eq.-5 ) THEN `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1989]` ` IF ( Tau0Dig2.eq.-1 ) THEN `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=2004]` ` IF (Tau0Dig1.eq.-6) THEN `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=2009]` ` IF (Tau0Dig1.eq.-7) THEN `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=2158]` ` IF(Depth.LE.10.) | fort.15 / ` NOUTE ` | 시계열 출력 형식 제어. 0=출력 없음. 정의 표의 모든 값은 다음 원문에 적었다. 조건: 공통.<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=848]` ` NOUTE = Number of elevation recording stations. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=850]` ` NOUTE =-3 Output is provided at the selected elevation recording stations in netCDF format. Following a hot start, a new fort.61 file is created. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=854]` ` NOUTE =-1 Output is provided at the selected elevation recording stations in standard ascii format. Following a hot start, a new fort.61 file is created. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=856]` ` NOUTE = 0 No output is provided at the selected elevation recording stations. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=858]` ` NOUTE = 1 Output is provided at the selected elevation recording stations in standard ascii format. Following a hot start, continued output is merged into the existing fort.61 file. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=862]` ` NOUTE = 3 Output is provided at the selected elevation recording stations in netCDF format. | fort.15 / ` ITITER ` | -1=lumped explicit GWCE. 1=iterative JCG.<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=1237]` ` ITITER = Parameters that provide information about the solver that will be used for the GWCE. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=1239]` ` ITITER = -1 only for lumped, explicit GWCE, matrix is diagonal and no external solver is needed `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=1241]` ` ITITER = 1 use iterative JCG solver (from ITPACKV 2D) `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=1261]` ` Note: all of the parameters must be input regardless of whether a diagonal or iterative solver is selected. However, ISLDIA, CONVCR and ITMAX are only used with the iterative solvers ` | 항상 읽지만 ITITER 선택 검사는 없다. GWCE는 ILump==0이면 JCG를 호출한다. 그 외에는 lumped 계산을 한다.<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4581]` ` READ(15,*) ITITER,ISLDIA,CONVCR,ITMAX `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=141]` ` IF (ILump.eq.0) THEN ! default, fully consistent LHS `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=145]` ` CALL DFAULT(IPARM,RPARM) `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=2000]` ` IF (ILump.eq.0) THEN ! default, fully consistent LHS `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=2003]` ` CALL JCG(NP,MNP,MNEI,NEITAB,COEF,GWCE_LV,ETAS, `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=2004]` ` &        IWKSP,NW,WKSP,IPARM,RPARM,IER) `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=2009]` ` ELSE ! lumped LHS ` | 문서가 ITITER를 솔버 선택으로 설명한다. 실제 분기는 IM에서 정한 ILump를 사용한다. |
| fort.15 / ` BCSTATIM ` | 경계 자료 시작 시각. 문서 단위 초. 상세 파일 조건은 인용 원문에 적었다.<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=1653]` ` Starting time (in seconds since ADCIRC cold start) for boundary condition data for the level of no motion boundary condition. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort15.rst line=159]` ` **[The following two lines will be read in if RES_BC_FLAG is negative.]** `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort15.rst line=162]` ` **[The following two lines will be read in if RES_BC_FLAG = 2.]** `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort15.rst line=165]` ` **[The following two lines will be read in if RES_BC_FLAG = 3.]** `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort15.rst line=168]` ` **[The following two lines will be read in if RES_BC_FLAG = 4.]** `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort15.rst line=171]` ` **[The following two lines will be read in if RES_BC_FLAG = 3 or 4 and BCFLAG_TEMP is not equal to 0.]** `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort15.rst line=172]` `` :ref:`TTBCTIMEINC <TTBCTIMEINC>`, :ref:`TTBCSTATIM <TTBCSTATIM>` `` | 조건 NOPE>0 AND (음수 RES_BC_FLAG 또는 양수1/2/3/4)에서 읽는다. 초 단위 내부 시각을 만들 때 86400을 곱한다.<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=5541]` ` IF ((RES_BC_FLAG.LT.0)) THEN  ! | fort.15 / ` metControl.rhoAir ` | 공기 밀도. 기본 1.15 kg/m³.<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2869]` ` Used to set the density of air; the default value is 1.15 kg/m^3. ` | REAL(8) 기본 1.293 kg/m³. 해당 READ는 부호 범위를 검사하지 않는다. 일반 namelist 오류 처리는 2.8.1에 적었다.<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=242]` ` NAMELIST /metControl/ WindDragLimit,DragLawString,outputWindDrag, `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=499]` ` read(unit=15,nml=metControl,iostat=ios,iomsg=ios_nml_error_msg) `<br>`[file=src/constants.F90 line=67]` ` real(8) :: RhoAir = 1.293d0 ` | 초기 밀도 1.293과 문서 기본값 1.15가 다르다. |
| fort.15 / ` waveCoupling.WaveWindMultiplier ` | 변수 이름의 fort.15 입력 정의를 찾지 못했다.<br> | REAL(8) 기본 1. waveCoupling에서 읽는다. 문서 철자 WindWaveMultiplier는 이 선언에 없다. 일반 namelist 오류 처리는 2.8.1에 적었다.<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=333]` ` NAMELIST /waveCoupling/ WaveWindMultiplier, `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=593]` ` read(unit=15,nml=waveCoupling,iostat=ios,iomsg=ios_nml_error_msg) `<br>`[file=src/constants.F90 line=119]` ` real(8) :: waveWindMultiplier = 1.0d0 ` | 문서에 WindWaveMultiplier라는 다른 철자만 있다. 2절의 철자 불일치에도 포함했다. |
| fort.15 / ` metControl.invertedBarometerOnElevationBoundary ` | 파일 형식은 timeBathyControl에 넣는다. Alaskan 예제는 metControl에 넣는다.<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort15.rst line=203]` `` :ref:`&timeBathyControl <timeBathyControl>` :ref:`NDDT=integerValue <NDDT>`, :ref:`BTIMINC=floatValue <BTIMINC>`, :ref:`BCHGTIMINC=floatValue <BCHGTIMINC>`, invertedBarometerOnElevationBoundary=logicalValue / ``<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/examples/alaskan_winter_storm_with_ice.rst line=59]` ` to 0.0025; set "invertedBarometerOnElevationBoundary" to true (in Alaska ` | LOGICAL 기본 FALSE. metControl에서 읽는다. timeBathyControl 선언에는 없다. 일반 namelist 오류 처리는 2.8.1에 적었다.<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=242]` ` NAMELIST /metControl/ WindDragLimit,DragLawString,outputWindDrag, `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=499]` ` read(unit=15,nml=metControl,iostat=ios,iomsg=ios_nml_error_msg) `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=114]` ` logical :: invertedBarometerOnElevationBoundary = .false. `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=242]` ` NAMELIST /metControl/ WindDragLimit,DragLawString,outputWindDrag, `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=243]` ` &  rhoAir, invertedBarometerOnElevationBoundary, `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=244]` ` &  nPowellSearchDomains `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=323]` ` NAMELIST /timebathycontrol/ NDDT,BTIMINC,BCHGTIMINC `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=499]` ` read(unit=15,nml=metControl,iostat=ios,iomsg=ios_nml_error_msg) ` | 현재 파일 형식의 namelist 소속이 코드와 다르다. 예제의 소속은 일치한다. |
| fort.15 / ` TVWControl.NOUT_TVW ` | 출력 형식 0/±1/±3/±4/±5. 미지정이면 NOUTGE를 상속한다.<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2989]` ` Integer that activates the production of the fort.77 output file as well as specifying its format; setting nout_TVW to zero turns off the production of the fort.77 output file while setting it to positive or negative 1, 3, 4, or 5 produce files in ASCII, netCDF3, sparse ASCII, or netCDF4, respectively. Due to the sparse nature of the weir height output data, sparse ASCII format (nout_TVW=4) is recommended. If use_TVW is set to .true. but this parameter nout_TVW is not set at all, then nout_TVW is set to the same value as the file format specifier for the fort.63 file (NOUTGE). The data stored in the fort.77 file represent the change in the weir height (in meters, positive upward) compared to the weir height specified in the ADCIRC mesh file, rather than the absolute height of the weir at a particular time. ` | 초기 sentinel -99999. USE_TVW이면 지정하지 않은 값을 NOUTGE/TOUTSGE/TOUTFGE/NSPOOLGE에서 가져온다. 이 대입은 해당 fort.15 전역 출력 줄 READ보다 앞선다. 이후 일정 계산은 STATIM을 뺀다. 읽기 오류 예외는 2.8.1에 적었다.<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=257]` ` NAMELIST /TVWControl/ USE_TVW,TVW_FILE,NOUT_TVW,TOUTS_TVW, `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=567]` ` READ(UNIT=15,NML=TVWControl,IOSTAT=IOS) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=563]` ` NOUT_TVW = -99999 `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=574]` ` IF(USE_TVW)THEN `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=575]` ` IF(NOUT_TVW.EQ.-99999D0)THEN `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=576]` ` NOUT_TVW=NOUTGE `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=577]` ` IF(TOUTS_TVW.EQ.-99999D0)TOUTS_TVW=TOUTSGE `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=578]` ` IF(TOUTF_TVW.EQ.-99999D0)TOUTF_TVW=TOUTFGE `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=579]` ` IF(NSPOOL_TVW.EQ.-99999D0)NSPOOL_TVW=NSPOOLGE `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=580]` ` ELSE `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=581]` ` IF(TOUTS_TVW.EQ.-99999D0)TOUTS_TVW=TOUTSGE `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=582]` ` IF(TOUTF_TVW.EQ.-99999D0)TOUTF_TVW=TOUTFGE `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=583]` ` IF(NSPOOL_TVW.EQ.-99999D0)NSPOOL_TVW=NSPOOLGE `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=584]` ` ENDIF `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=585]` ` ELSE `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=586]` ` NOUT_TVW = 0 `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4132]` ` READ(15,*) NOUTGE,TOUTSGE,TOUTFGE,NSPOOLGE `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4279]` ` NTCYS_TVW=INT((TOUTS_TVW-STATIM)*(86400.D0/DTDP) + 0.5d0) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4280]` ` NTCYF_TVW=INT((TOUTF_TVW-STATIM)*(86400.D0/DTDP) + 0.5d0) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4282]` ` IF(NSPOOL_TVW.EQ.0)THEN `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4283]` ` NDSETS_TVW = 0 `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4285]` ` NDSETS_TVW=INT((NTCYF_TVW-NTCYS_TVW)/NSPOOL_TVW) ` | 해석: 상속 대입은 NOUTGE 위치 입력을 읽기 전에 수행한다. 그 뒤 전역 출력 READ로 갱신한 값을 다시 상속하는 대입은 없다. 실제 위치 입력의 기본 일정 상속을 보장하지 않는다. |

If this is not satisfied, the run will terminate. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=28]` ` Internal barrier height (positive above the geoid and negative below the geoid) at node NBVV(k,j) and the paired node IBCONN(k,j)). The barrier height must be greater than the bathymetric depth at these nodes, i.e., recalling the different sign convention between the bathymetric depth and the barrier height, BARINHT(k,j) > –DP(NBVV(k,j) and <b >BARINHT(k,j) > –DP(IBCONN(k,j)). If this is not satisfied, the run will terminate. ` | `[file=src/mesh.F line=2608]` ` IF(BARLANHT(JGW).LT.-DP(NBV(JGW))) THEN `<br>`[file=src/mesh.F line=2627]` ` IF(BARINHT(JGW).LT.-DP(NBV(JGW))) THEN `<br>`[file=src/mesh.F line=2646]` ` IF(BARINHT(JGW).LT.-DP(NBV(JGW))) THEN ` | 문서는 높이가 지형고보다 반드시 커야 한다고 적는다. 코드는 높이가 지형고보다 작은 경우에만 종료한다. 코드는 두 값이 같은 경우를 허용한다. |

| F19-01 / fort.19 읽기 조건 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort19.rst line=6]` `` The fort.19 file contains non-periodic, time varying elevation boundary conditions for "elevation specified" boundary nodes. | F19-05 / ETIMINC 반복 여부 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort19.rst line=20]` ` Repeat the block above for each time step until the end of the simulation. ` | `[file=src/cstart.F line=208]` ` READ(19,*) ETIMINC `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=210]` ` READ(19,*) ESBIN1(J) `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=213]` ` READ(19,*) ESBIN2(J) `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=884]` ` ETIME1=ETIME2 `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=885]` ` ETIME2=ETIME1+ETIMINC `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=888]` ` READ(19,*) ESBIN2(J) ` | 문서의 ‘Repeat the block above’는 ETIMINC까지 포함한다. 코드는 파일 처음에서 ETIMINC를 한 번만 읽는다. 문서의 예제 36–45줄은 코드의 한 번 읽기와 맞는다. |
| F20-01 / 읽기 조건 NFFR | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort20.rst line=6]` `` The fort.20 file contains non-periodic, normal flow boundary conditions for "specified non-zero normal flow" boundary nodes. This file is only read when a "specified non-zero normal flow" boundary condition has been specified in the :doc:`Grid and Boundary Information File <fort14>` (IBTYPE=2, 12, or 22) and NFFR=0 in the :doc:`Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File <fort15>`. ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=811]` ` Number of frequencies in the specified normal flow external boundary condition. If NFFR=0 or NFFR=-1, the normal flow boundary condition is assumed to be non-periodic and will be read in from the Non-periodic, Normal Flux Boundary Condition File. If NFFR=0, ADCIRC assumes that the flux data in that file start at the cold start time; but if NFFR=-1, ADCIRC assumes that the flux data in that file start at the hot start time. On the other hand, positive integer values of NFFR indicate the number of frequency components that make up the periodic flux boundaries. ` | `[file=src/cstart.F line=226]` ` IF(NFLUXF.EQ.1.AND.NFFR.LE.0)THEN `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=981]` ` IF((NFLUXF.EQ.1).AND.(NFFR.EQ.-1)) THEN `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=987]` ` OPEN(20,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.20') ` | fort20.rst는 NFFR=0만 적는다. 코드는 NFFR=-1의 핫스타트 기준 자료도 읽는다. parameter_definitions는 이 확장 조건을 설명한다. |
| F20-06 / FTIMINC 반복 여부 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort20.rst line=20]` ` Repeat the block above for each time step until the end of the simulation. ` | `[file=src/cstart.F line=233]` ` READ(20,*) FTIMINC `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=239]` ` READ(20,*) QNIN_TMP `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=248]` ` READ(20,*) QNIN_TMP ` | 코드는 FTIMINC를 파일 처음에서 한 번만 읽는다. 반복 자료는 유량 레코드다. |
| F24-02 / Alpha line | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort24.rst line=18]` ` Alpha line `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort24.rst line=30]` ` 1. The first four lines (Alpha line, Constituent frequency, 1, Constituent name) for each constituent must be present in the file but they are skipped over during the ADCIRC read. ` | `[file=src/read_input.F line=6468]` ` READ(24, *) dmy_char, dmy_char ` | 문서는 알파 한 레코드를 건너뛴다고 적는다. 코드는 같은 읽기문에서 문자값 두 개를 요구한다. 공백 없는 단일 값 레코드는 다음 레코드까지 소비할 수 있다. |
| F24-04 / Constituent name·순서 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort24.rst line=21]` ` Constituent name (e.g., M2) `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort24.rst line=30]` ` 1. The first four lines (Alpha line, Constituent frequency, 1, Constituent name) for each constituent must be present in the file but they are skipped over during the ADCIRC read. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort24.rst line=32]` `` 2. Entries must be grouped by constituents and must be in the same order as the tidal potential terms listed in the :ref:`Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File <fort15>`. `` | `[file=src/read_input.F line=6471]` ` READ(24, '(A)') const_name `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=6477]` ` IF(TRIM(TIPOTAG(J)).EQ.TRIM(const_name))THEN `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=6478]` ` IDX = J `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=6482]` ` IF(IDX.EQ.-1)THEN `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=6486]` ` call terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, message=scratchMessage) ` | 문서는 성분 이름을 건너뛰고 fort.15와 같은 순서를 요구한다. 모델 ASCII reader는 이름으로 TIPOTAG를 찾는다. 모델 reader는 알 수 없는 이름에서 종료한다. 해석: 모델 reader에는 문서의 동일 순서 조건이 필요하지 않다. prep의 순서 검사는 F24-08에 적었다. |
| NA-01 / primitive_weighting_in_continuity_equation: 단위 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=64]` ` **Description**: Same as existing TAU0. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=68]` ` **Units**: Unitless. (Units for nodal attributes are specified by the user). `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=70]` ` **Number of values per node**: 1. `<br>`[file=models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/wiki/markdown/Fort.13_file.md line=180]` `Units: Specified by the user, 1/time.` | `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1214]` ` READ(13,'(A80)') Tau0Units `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1215]` ` READ(13,*) Tau0NoOfVals `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1216]` ` READ(13,*) Tau0DefVal `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=497]` ` Tau0DefVal             = 0.0 `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1919]` ` &           /,5X,'Tau0 = ',E15.8,2X,'1/sec',/) ` | rst는 'Unitless'라고 적고 같은 줄에 단위는 사용자가 지정한다고 적는다. 위키는 'Specified by the user, 1/time.'이라고 적는다. 불일치는 rst와 위키 사이의 차이이다. 코드는 Tau0Units를 A80 문자열로 읽기만 한다(nodalattr.F:1214). nodalattr.F:1919의 '1/sec' 로그는 노드 속성을 읽지 않을 때(.not.LoadTau0) 쓰는 fort.15 상수 Tau0의 로그이므로 노드 속성 단위의 근거가 아니다. 초기값은 0이다. |
| NA-06 / quadratic_friction_coefficient_at_sea_floor | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=111]` ` **Description**: See the description of CF in the Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File. If the user elects to load this from the fort.13 file, NOLIBF must be set to 1 or the run will terminate. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=115]` ` **Units**: Unitless. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=117]` ` **Number of values per node**: 1. ` | `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1224]` ` READ(13,'(A80)') QuadraticFricUnits `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1225]` ` READ(13,*) QuadraticFricNoOfVals `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1226]` ` READ(13,*) QuadraticFricDefVal `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1387]` ` CALL LoadAttrVec(FRIC, QuadraticFricDefVal, `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=506]` ` QuadraticFricDefVal    = 0.0 `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1850]` ` IF ((LoadChezy.or.LoadManningsN.or.LoadQuadraticFric).and. `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1851]` ` &     NoLiBF.ne.1.AND.NOLIBF.NE.3) THEN ` | 코드는 무차원 계수 하나를 읽는다. 초기값은 0이다. 문서는 NOLIBF=1을 의무로 적는다. 검사 조건은 NOLIBF=3도 허용한다. |
| NA-09 / bridge_pilings_friction_parameters: 속성 이름 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=153]` ` bridge_pilings_friction_paramenters – BK, BAlpha, BDelX, POAN `<br>`[file=models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/wiki/markdown/Fort.13_file.md line=79]` `Nodal attribute name: bridge_pilings_friction_parameters` | `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1096]` ` CASE("bridge_pilings_friction_parameters") `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1244]` ` CASE("bridge_pilings_friction_parameters") ` | rst 이름 paramenters는 코드의 parameters와 다르다. 위키 이름은 코드와 같다. rst 이름을 그대로 지정하면 해당 속성을 선택하지 않는다. |
| NA-12 / mannings_n_at_sea_floor: NOLIBF·0 이하 값 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=174]` ` **Description**: Manning's n. If the user elects to use this nodal attribute, NOLIBF must be set to 1 or the run will terminate. During execution, the Manning's n value specified here is converted to an equivalent quadratic friction coefficient before the bottom stress is calculated. The equivalent quadratic friction coefficient is calculated according to the following formula at each node at each time step: Cd(t)=(g*n^2)/cuberoot(depth[+eta(t)]) where depth is the bathymetric depth and [eta(t)] is the water surface elevation. The addition of the water surface elevation is conditional upon the setting of NOLIFA: eta(t) is treated as zero if NOLIFA is set to zero in the fort.15 file. Finally, the value of CF in the fort.15 is used to set a lower limit on the resulting equivalent quadratic friction coefficient, since the Cd calculated from this formula tends to become small in deep water. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=182]` ` **Values**: Greater than zero. ` | `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1850]` ` IF ((LoadChezy.or.LoadManningsN.or.LoadQuadraticFric).and. `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1851]` ` &     NoLiBF.ne.1.AND.NOLIBF.NE.3) THEN `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=2423]` ` IF (ManningsN(I).le.0d0) cycle ! Can use Cd where man <= 0 ` | 문서는 NOLIBF=1과 양수를 적는다. 코드는 NOLIBF=3을 허용한다. 일반 비 subgrid 경로는 0 이하 n에서 기존 Cd를 유지한다. |
| NA-13 / chezy_friction_coefficient_at_sea_floor | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=189]` ` **Description**: Chezy friction coefficient. If the user elects to use this nodal attribute, NOLIBF must be set to 1 or the run will terminate. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=193]` ` **Units**: Specified by user. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=195]` ` **Number of values per node**: 1. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=197]` ` **Values**: Greater than zero. ` | `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1262]` ` READ(13,'(A80)') ChezyUnits `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1263]` ` READ(13,*) ChezyNoOfVals `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1264]` ` READ(13,*) ChezyDefVal `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=510]` ` ChezyDefVal            = 0.0 `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1850]` ` IF ((LoadChezy.or.LoadManningsN.or.LoadQuadraticFric).and. `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1851]` ` &     NoLiBF.ne.1.AND.NOLIBF.NE.3) THEN `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=2435]` ` FRIC(I) = G/(Chezy(I)**2) ` | 코드는 값 하나를 읽는다. 초기값은 0이다. 코드는 G/(Chezy(I)**2)로 변환한다. 문서의 NOLIBF=1 의무와 달리 검사 조건은 3도 허용한다. 양수 조건을 읽기문은 검사하지 않는다. |
| NA-16 / average_horizontal_eddy_viscosity_in_sea_water_wrt_depth: 변수·전역 대체값 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=231]` ` average_horizontal_eddy_viscosity_in_sea_water_wrt_depth – EVC `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=234]` ` **Description**: See description of ESLC in the Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=238]` ` **Units**: Specified by the user. ((length**2)/time). `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=240]` ` **Number of values per node**: 1. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=242]` ` **Values**: Greater than or equal to zero. ` | `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1273]` ` READ(13,'(A80)') EVMUnits `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1274]` ` READ(13,*) EVMNoOfVals `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1275]` ` READ(13,*) EVMDefVal `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=513]` ` EVMDefVal              = 0.0 `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1456]` ` CALL LoadAttrVec(EVM, EVMDefVal, `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=2114]` ` EVM(I)=ESLM ` | rst는 EVC와 ESLC를 적는다. 코드는 EVM과 ESLM을 사용한다. 초기값은 0이다. 파일 기본값을 사용자가 지정한다. |

| NA-21 / initial_river_elevation | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=283]` `` **Description**: The initial_river_elevation nodal attribute is used to set the initial water surface elevation in inland rivers that have some portion of the river bed above mean sea level as well as an upstream flux boundary condition. ADCIRC assumes by default that vertices that are above mean sea level (i.e., with negative depths) will be dry when the simulation starts. This assumption is violated when the elevation of a river bed is above mean sea level, and there is an upstream flux boundary. This nodal attribute is used in those cases to provide the initial water surface elevation of the river at cold start. See also :ref:`initial_river_elevation_description`. ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=285]` ` **Requirement**: Required only if the domain contains a river with an upstream flux boundary condition and a bed elevation above mean sea level. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=287]` ` **Units**: Length. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=289]` ` **Values**: Any. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=291]` ` **ADCIRC Variable**: Eta2. `<br>`[file=models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/wiki/markdown/Fort.13_file.md line=138]` `Requirement: Optional, but recommended if the domain contains a river with an upstream flux boundary condition and a bed elevation above ADCIRC's starting water elevation (zero unless otherwise specified).`<br>`[file=models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/wiki/markdown/Fort.13_file.md line=141]` ``ADCIRC Variable: `River_et_WSE`.`` | `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1314]` ` READ(13,'(A80)') River_et_WSEUnits `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1315]` ` READ(13,*) River_et_WSENoOfVals `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1316]` ` READ(13,*) River_et_WSEDefVal `<br>`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=520]` ` River_et_WSEDefVal     = 0.d0 `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1156]` ` IF (LoadRiver_et_WSE.eqv..true.) then `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1182]` ` if (LoadRiver_et_WSE.eqv..true.) then `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1183]` ` where (River_et_WSE.GT.0.d0) `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1184]` ` eta2 = eta2 + River_et_WSE `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1188]` ` deallocate (River_et_WSE) ` | 코드는 River_et_WSE를 읽는다. 초기값은 0이다. 코드는 양수 값만 기존 ETA2에 더한다. 문서의 Any와 단순 초기 수면 지정 설명은 이 조건을 적지 않는다. 위키는 속성을 Optional로 적는다. 코드는 속성 존재를 하상 높이로 강제하지 않는다. |

(IDEN.EQ.2 .OR. IDEN.EQ.3 .OR. `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=962]` ` &    IDEN.EQ.4)) THEN `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=963]` ` OPEN(39,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.39', ` | 코드는 IBTYPE 102·112·122·152를 river 경계로 분류한다. 읽기 조건은 IDEN=2·3·4다. 문서는 122와 모든 양수 IDEN으로 설명한다. 경계 배열 할당의 추가 제한은 F14-30에 적었다. |

| F14-30 / IBTYPE=102·112·122: 읽기와 할당의 차이 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=113]` ` = 102, 112, or 122 flux specified baroclinic. In order to designate a river boundary as baroclinic, 100 should be added to the IBTYPE that would be appropriate in the barotropic case. For example, to convert a barotropic river boundary (IBTYPE of 22) to a baroclinic river boundary with freshwater inflow, change the IBTYPE to 122. If there is a 1 in the 100s place of the IBTYPE, ADCIRC will then try to read an input file (fort.39) for the salinity and/or temperature boundary condition. The format of the fort.39 file depends on the value of IDEN; see the documentation of the fort.39 file for more details. ` | `[file=src/mesh.F line=446]` ` case(0,1,2,10,11,12,20,21,22,30,32,52,102,112,122,152) `<br>`[file=src/mesh.F line=1405]` ` case(102,112,122,152) `<br>`[file=src/boundaries.F line=353]` ` case(0,1,2,10,11,12,20,21,22,30,32,52,94,122) `<br>`[file=src/boundaries.F line=354]` ` allocate(simpleFluxBoundaries(sfCount)%nodes(nvell(i))) ` | mesh 읽기 분기는 102·112·122를 갖는다. simpleFluxBoundaries 할당 분기는 이 셋 중 122만 갖는다. 해석: 102·112 지원을 읽기 분기만으로 확정하면 안 된다. 모델을 실행하지 않았다. |

| NA-28 / EVM의 Smagorinsky 사용 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=238]` ` **Units**: Specified by the user. ((length**2)/time). `<br>`[file=models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/wiki/markdown/Fort.13_file.md line=101]` `Units: For standard eddy viscosity, units are specified by the user,((length**2)/time).  For Smagorinsky-type, unitless.` | `[file=src/gwce.F line=828]` ` EVMEle=NCEle*(EVM(NM1)+EVM(NM2)+EVM(NM3))/3.d0 `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=831]` ` IF ((CSmag_Eh).or.(Smag_Comp_Flag)) THEN `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=832]` ` EVMSmag=EVMEle* `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=833]` ` &             sqrt((LSXXGradA-LSYYGradA)*(LSXXGradA-LSYYGradA) `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=834]` ` &                 +(LSYXGradA+LSXYGradA)*(LSYXGradA+LSXYGradA)) `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=837]` ` IF (EVMSmag .gt. SMAG_UPPER_LIM) EVMSmag = SMAG_UPPER_LIM `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=838]` ` IF (EVMSmag .lt. SMAG_LOWER_LIM) EVMSmag = SMAG_LOWER_LIM `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=840]` ` EVMEle=EVMSmag ` | 코드는 조건을 만족하면 EVM 평균에 변형률 관련 항을 곱한다. 해석: 이 경로의 입력은 무차원 Smagorinsky 계수다. rst의 단위 설명은 이 경로를 누락한다. NA-17과 같은 단위 쟁점의 소비 근거다. |
| NA-29 / elemental_slope_limiter의 부호·활성 상태 지속 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=249]` ` **Description**: This nodal attribute is used to selectively limit the maximum elevation gradient that can occur across an element, thus improving numerical stability. Alternatively, it is also capable of merely logging individual elements where a specified elemental slope is exceeded at some point during the simulation. When this nodal attribute is loaded, warning messages will be written to the screen and to the fort.16 log file whenever the elevation gradient meets or exceeds the threshold value for the first time at a particular node. Once the elevation gradient is met or exceeded at a node, the elemental slope limiter remains active at that node for the remainder of the ADCIRC run. When the elemental slope limiter is active at a node, the water surface elevation at that node is reset to the average of the water surface elevations of the surrounding nodes. At the end of the run, a file called ESLNodes.63 will be written to indicate the nodes where the threshold elevation gradient was met or exceeded. Furthermore, If there is an ESLNodes.63 file in the input directory when ADCIRC starts, ADCIRC will load the existing ESLNodes.63 file along with the hotstart file so that the simulation can pick up where it left off, in terms of the elements where the slope is actively limited. When such a simulation finishes, it will overwrite the existing ESLNodes.63 file with a new one that reflects the updated state of limited elemental slopes. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst line=257]` ` **Values**: Zero indicates that slope limiting is always active at that node, because a zero elevation gradient will always be met or exceeded; a positive value indicates the maximum gradient to be allowed at that node, at or beyond which the slope limiter is activated; a negative number indicates that the elevation gradients should be compared to the absolute value of the nodal attribute at this node. In the case of a negative number, ADCIRC will log a warning to the screen and to the fort.16 file the first time the elemental slope is exceeded, but ESLNodes.63 file will not be affected, and the elemental slope limiting will not actually occur at the node. A suggested value for this nodal attribute is 0.001. ` | `[file=src/gwce.F line=3185]` ` grad_check: IF (dEta2Mag.GE. `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=3186]` ` &           ABS(elemental_slope_limiter_grad_max(NM(IE,I)))) THEN `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=3194]` ` grad_case: if (elemental_slope_limiter_grad_max(nm(ie,i)) `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=3195]` ` &               .ge.0.0d0) then `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=3204]` ` eslcount(nm(ie,i)) = 1 ! for output file `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=3206]` ` elemental_slope_limiter_active(NM(IE,I)) = .true. `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=3210]` ` IF (elemental_slope_limiter_max_exceeded(NM(IE,I)) `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=3216]` ` elemental_slope_limiter_max_exceeded(NM(IE,I)) = `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=3217]` ` &                      .true. `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=3129]` ` ! jgf51.47: Per Rick's specification, start off by deactivating `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=3130]` ` ! any slope limitation triggered in the previous time step; `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=3131]` ` ! we have a fresh start each time step in determining whether `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=3132]` ` ! to activate slope limiting at each node. `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=3135]` ` elemental_slope_limiter_active(:) = .false. ` | 코드는 절댓값 기준과 >=를 사용한다. 0 이상 값은 active 상태를 켠다. 음수 값은 첫 초과 로그 상태만 켠다. 문서는 활성 상태가 실행 종료까지 남는다고 적는다. 코드는 check_slopes 호출마다 active 배열을 .false.로 초기화한다. 초과 이력인 eslcount와 현재 active 상태를 구분해야 한다. ESLNodes.63 재입력은 NA-38에서 따로 판정한다. |
