| NWS-heading / v53 얼음+fort.24 파랑 표 머리 | `[file=models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/website_markdown/home/documentation/users-manual-v53/input-file-descriptions/nws-values-table/index.md line=408]` `\| Meteorological Data Format \| Met. Only  Waves Off \| Met. plus Waves from fort.23 \| Met. plus Waves SWAN \| Met. plus Waves STWAVE \| Met. plus Ice Coverage OWI-like format plus Waves from fort.24 \| Met. plus Ice Coverage OWI-like format plus Waves from SWAN \| Met. plus Ice Coverage OWI-like format plus Waves from STWAVE \|` | `[file=src/cstart.F line=312]` ` IF(NRS.EQ.1) THEN `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=313]` ` OPEN(23,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.23') ` | v53 표 머리는 얼음과 fort.24 파랑을 적는다. v56.2.1의 NRS=1 읽기 파일은 fort.23이다. fort.24는 SAL 입력이다. |
| MET-02 / NWS=2: WTIMINC 레코드 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=119]` `` :ref:`WTIMINC <WTIMINC>` ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=124]` ` Repeat the block above for each time step until the end of the simulation. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=133]` ` * Additional sets of met. data must be provided every WTIMINC, where WTIMINC is the met. data time interval and is specified in the Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File. ` | `[file=src/wind.F line=1816]` ` OPEN(22,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.22') `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=1817]` ` READ(22,*) (NHG,WVNX1(I),WVNY1(I),PRN1(I),I=1,NP) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=1818]` ` READ(22,*) (NHG,WVNX2(I),WVNY2(I),PRN2(I),I=1,NP) ` | rst 형식은 fort.22에 WTIMINC 레코드를 적는다. 문서의 설명 문장은 fort.15에 WTIMINC를 지정하도록 적는다. 코드는 fort.22에서 WTIMINC를 읽지 않는다. |
| MET-05 / NWS=3의 좌표계 의무 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=170]` ` * Meteorological data is input to a longitude, latitude grid and interpolated in space onto the ADCIRC grid. The ADCIRC grid must be in lon, lat coordinates. ` | `[file=src/wind.F line=3326]` ` IF(ICS.NE.1) THEN `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3327]` ` YCOOR=SFEA(I)*RAD2DEG `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3328]` ` XCOOR=SLAM(I)*RAD2DEG `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3330]` ` IF(ICS.EQ.1) THEN `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3331]` ` YCOOR=Y(I) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3332]` ` XCOOR=X(I) ` | 문서는 ADCIRC 격자가 lon·lat이어야 한다고 적는다. 코드는 ICS=1에서 Cartesian X·Y를 사용한 별도 보간 경로도 갖는다. 이 차이는 입력 허용 경로의 차이다. |
| MET-07 / NWS=±4: PRN의 단위 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=217]` ` * Wind velocity (assumed to be 10m 10 minute averaged value) must be input in knots and surface atmospheric pressure must be input in hundredths of a millibar. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=224]` ` * The following relationship is used in ADCIRC to convert to pressure in meters of water from pressure in hundredths of a millibar `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=225]` ` * PRESSURE{m H2O}=PRESSURE{Pa/100}*100/(GRAVITY*DENSITY H2O). ` | `[file=src/wind.F line=3411]` ` PRN2(NHG)=100.d0*PRN2(NHG)/RHOWATG !CONVERT MILLIBARS TO M OF WATER `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3412]` ` IF(PRN2(NHG).EQ.0.) PRN2(NHG)=101300.d0/RHOWATG                       !RAL0315+ OK ` | 문서는 hundredths of a millibar를 적는다. 코드 주석은 MILLIBARS를 적는다. 코드는 입력값에 100을 곱한 뒤 RHOWATG로 나눈다. 해석: 이 변환이 요구하는 입력은 mbar다. |
| MET-09 / NWS=6: 날짜·시각 여섯 필드 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=274]` ` IMDAY, IMMONTH, IMYEAR, IMHOUR, IMMIN, IMSEC ` | `[file=src/wind.F line=3520]` ` DO I=1,NWLAT `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3521]` ` DO J=1,NWLON `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3522]` ` READ(22,*) WVXFN(I,J),WVYFN(I,J),PRN(I,J) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3523]` ` END DO `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3524]` ` END DO ` | 코드는 첫 레코드부터 U·V·P를 읽는다. fort.22에서 IMDAY·IMMONTH·IMYEAR·IMHOUR·IMMIN·IMSEC를 읽지 않는다. |
| MET-13 / NWS=8: LatN/S·LongE/W | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=342]` ` AL, ##, YYYY, MM/DD/HH24, TECH, TAU, LatN/S, LongE/W, VMAX, MSLP, TY, ... `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=353]` ` * The symmetric vortex model (NWS=8) in ADCIRC assumes that the longitudes in the fort.22 are west longitude, so it multiplies the longitude values by -1. It ignores the 'E' or 'W' in the longitude column of the fort.22. ` | `[file=src/nws08.F90 line=946]` ` CastType(i), iFcstInc(i), iLat(i), ns(i), iLon(i), ew(i), & `<br>`[file=src/nws08.F90 line=1047]` ` Lat(i) = Lat(i)/10.d0 ! convert 10ths of degs to degs `<br>`[file=src/nws08.F90 line=1048]` ` Lon(i) = Lon(i)/10.d0 ! convert 10ths of degs to degs `<br>`[file=src/nws08.F90 line=1049]` ` if (ew(i) == 'W') then `<br>`[file=src/nws08.F90 line=1050]` ` Lon(i) = -1.d0*Lon(i) ! negative b/c WEST longitude `<br>`[file=src/nws08.F90 line=1052]` ` if (ns(i) == 'S') then `<br>`[file=src/nws08.F90 line=1053]` ` Lat(i) = -1.d0*Lat(i) ! negative b/c SOUTH latitude ` | 문서는 E/W를 무시하고 서경으로 고정한다고 적는다. 코드는 W일 때 경도 부호를 바꾼다. 코드는 S일 때 위도 부호를 바꾼다. |
| MET-16 / NWS=8: 모델 이름 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=330]` ` NWS = 8 or 108: Dynamic Symmetric Holland Vortex Model `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=333]` ` In this format, a single tropical cyclone model using the Generalized asymmetric Holland formulation with a moving reference frame and a background wind field is used. ` | `[file=src/nws08.F90 line=531]` ` call HollandGet(WVNX, WVNY, PRESS, TIMELOC, FoundEye, EyeLon, EyeLat) `<br>`[file=src/nws08.F90 line=562]` ` subroutine HollandGet(WVNX, WVNY, PRESS, TIMELOC, FoundEye, EyeLon, EyeLat) ` | rst 본문은 Generalized asymmetric Holland로 설명한다. 코드의 NWS8 루틴은 HollandGet을 호출한다. GAHM은 MET-18의 NWS=20에서 처리한다. |
| MET-32 / NWS=15·16: 입력 디렉터리 | `[file=docs/user_guide/running_adcirc/command_line_options.rst line=11]` ``` ``-I INPUTDIR`` ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/running_adcirc/command_line_options.rst line=12]` ` Look for input files in the directory 'INPUTDIR' instead of looking in the directory where the ADCIRC command was issued. ` | `[file=src/wind.F line=6258]` ` call openFileForRead(22,trim(globaldir)//"/fort.22",errorio) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=6971]` ` call openFileForRead(22,trim(globaldir)//"/fort.22",errorio) ` | -I의 일반 입력 디렉터리 설명과 달리 이 두 초기화 루틴은 globaldir에서 fort.22를 연다. CLI-01의 예외이다. |
| MET-34 / NWS=15: HWind 격자 간격 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=496]` ` * the mercator grid spacing is in meters and is uniform in x and y (dx=dy) ` | `[file=src/wind.F line=6723]` ` read(220,'(7X,F8.5,A)',end=246,err=248,iostat=errorio) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=6724]` ` &   hWindFiles(cn)%dh `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=6725]` ` write(scratchMessage,'("Mercator grid spacing is ",F8.5," km.")') `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=6787]` ` hWindFiles(cn)%dh = hWindFiles(cn)%dh * 1000.d0 ` | 문서는 입력 격자 간격을 m로 적는다. 코드는 km로 읽고 1000을 곱한다. |
| MET-38 / NWS=16: 예제의 Pc 열 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=535]` `` :ref:`hours(i) <hours>` :ref:`rampMult(i) <rampMult>` :ref:`filename(i) <filename>` ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=569]` ` 0.0 -1 0.0 "/home/jason/hwind/al092011_0828_1330" ! time (hours), Pc (mb), ramp mult, filename `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=570]` ` 6.0 -1 0.5 "/home/jason/hwind/al092011_0828_1930" ` | `[file=src/wind.F line=7009]` ` read(22,*,end=246,err=248,iostat=errorio) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7010]` ` &     GFDL_Files(i)%cycleTime, `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7011]` ` &     GFDL_Files(i)%rampVal, `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7012]` ` &     GFDL_Files(i)%file_name ` | 예제는 시간·Pc·ramp·파일 이름 네 필드를 적는다. 형식 정의와 코드는 시간·ramp·파일 이름 세 필드를 사용한다. |
| MET-42 / NWS=16: NCELLS의 형식 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=545]` ` * Line 1: Number of grid cells (f10.4) NCELLS ` | `[file=src/wind.F line=7251]` ` !       READ(LUN,'(F10.4)') RPTS_GFDL `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7252]` ` !       NPTS_GFDL = INT(RPTS_GFDL) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7253]` ` read(LUN,'(I10)') NPTS_GFDL ` | 문서는 F10.4를 적는다. 현재 코드는 I10을 사용한다. F10.4를 읽는 옛 줄은 주석이다. |
| F200-05 / NWS=11: UE·VE·PE의 저장 순서 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst line=27]` `` for j=1, :ref:`LONB <LONB>` * :ref:`LATB <LATB>` ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst line=28]` `` :ref:`UE(j) <UE>`, :ref:`VE(j) <VE>`, :ref:`PE(j) <PE>` ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst line=29]` ` end j loop ` | `[file=src/wind.F line=4196]` ` READ(IEXT,END=1100) UE,VE,PE `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=4201]` ` READ(IEXT,END=1100) UE,VE,PE ` | 문서는 각 j에 UE(j)·VE(j)·PE(j)를 나란히 적는다. 코드는 한 unformatted 레코드에서 UE 전체·VE 전체·PE 전체 순서로 읽는다. |
| F200-06 / NWS=11: binary OPEN의 FORM | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst line=81]` ` - File type: binary (ACCESS='sequential', FORM='unformatted') ` | `[file=src/wind.F line=4187]` ` call openFileForRead(IEXT,FNAME1,errorIO) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=4195]` ` READ(IEXT,END=1100) IYEAR,IMONTH,IDAY,IHOUR `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=4196]` ` READ(IEXT,END=1100) UE,VE,PE `<br>`[file=src/io.F90 line=100]` ` open (lun, file=trim(filename), status='OLD', & `<br>`[file=src/io.F90 line=101]` ` action='READ', iostat=errorIO) ` | 문서는 FORM='unformatted'를 요구한다. 실제 호출한 openFileForRead는 FORM을 지정하지 않는다. 해석: 기본 formatted 연결과 unformatted READ가 충돌한다. 실행 결과는 확인하지 않음. |
| F200-07 / NWS=11: 기압 단위 주장 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst line=84]` ` - Surface pressure: millibars `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst line=101]` ` PRESSURE{m H2O} = PRESSURE{millibars}/(100*GRAVITY*DENSITY of H2O) ` | `[file=src/wind.F line=4165]` ` RHOWATG100=RHOWAT0*G*100.d0 `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=4227]` ` PRN2(N)=P1*D1(N)+P2*D2(N)+P3*D3(N) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=4228]` ` PRN2(N)=PRN2(N)/RHOWATG100 ` | 문서는 입력 기압을 millibar로 적는다. 문서의 식과 코드는 기압을 100*RHOWAT0*G로 나눈다. 해석: millibar를 Pa로 바꾸는 100배 변환과 반대이다. NWS=16의 millibar 변환은 MET-43에 적었다. |
| F200-09 / NWS=11: WTIMINC당 dataset 수 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst line=74]` ` - Each file contains 8 data sets spaced 3 hours apart (WTIMINC = 10800 sec) `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst line=109]` ` - For NWS=11, each file contains 8 data sets for a total of 1 day of data per file `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst line=111]` ` - For NWS=11, 8 sets of meteorological data must be provided every WTIMINC in each file ` | `[file=src/wind.F line=4205]` ` 1100 IF(IDSETFLG.EQ.8) CLOSE(IEXT) ` | 문서 마지막 문장은 WTIMINC마다 여덟 dataset을 요구한다. 같은 문서의 앞부분은 WTIMINC 간격의 dataset 여덟 개를 하루 파일에 요구한다. 코드는 IDSETFLG=8에서 파일을 닫는다. |

An oceanographic convention is used where velocity is positive when it is blowing toward positive coordinate directions. Units are assumed to be m/s ` | `[file=src/wind.F line=3652]` ` READ(22,*) WVXFN(K,J),WVYFN(K,J),PRN(K,J) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=1155]` ` WindX = WVNX1(I) + WTRatio*(WVNX2(I)-WVNX1(I)) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=1156]` ` WindY = WVNY1(I) + WTRatio*(WVNY2(I)-WVNY1(I)) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=1157]` ` WSX2(I) = RampMete*WindX !apply ramp `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=1158]` ` WSY2(I) = RampMete*WindY `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3691]` ` PRN2(I) = PRN2(I)/RHOWATG ` | fort22.rst 제목은 바람 응력을 적는다. 공통 매개변수 정의는 NWS=±7의 WVXFN·WVYFN을 m/s 풍속으로 적는다. 코드는 값을 응력으로 직접 사용한다. 해석: 두 필드는 물 기준 밀도로 나눈 응력 단위를 요구한다. |

| ICE-01 / 데이터 파일 이름 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort25.rst line=3]` ` Fort.25, 255/277: Ice Coverage Input Files `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort25.rst line=21]` ` If numIceFields in the fort.25 file is set to 1, only the fort.225 file will be read; if numIceFields in the fort.25 file is set to 2 then both the fort.225 and fort.227 files will be read. The fort.225 (the basin scale, i.e., large scale coarse grid) and optional fort.227 (the region scale, i.e., smaller scale finer grid) files contain the actual ice percentage values (0.0 to 100.0%) and a value of -1.0 which means land or no data available. A value of -1.0% is treated as 0.0% ice in ADCIRC. ` | `[file=src/owi_ice.F line=132]` ` OPEN(225,FILE=TRIM(GBLINPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.225', STATUS='OLD') `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=158]` ` OPEN(227,FILE=TRIM(GBLINPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.227',STATUS='OLD') ` | 문서 제목은 255/277을 적는다. 본문과 코드는 225/227을 사용한다. |
| ICE-02 / 얼음 flag의 NWS 배치 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort25.rst line=8]` ` In order to use ice field data, two changes are required to an existing fort.15: the ten thousands place must be set to 12 (see the NWS Simplified Table of Values for details), and the time increment of the ice field data (CICE_TIMINC) in seconds must be supplied on the WTIMINC line (see the Supplemental Meteorology/Wave/Ice Parameters table for details). Ice coverage datasets are linearly interpolated in time by ADCIRC, as a result, at least two datasets are required. ` | `[file=src/read_input.F line=1775]` ` NCICE = INT(ABS(NWS)/1000) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1776]` ` NWS = INT(ABS(NWS)-NCICE*1000)*INT(NWS/ABS(NWS))  !RESETTING NWS/NRS ` | 문서는 ten thousands place를 12로 설정한다고 적는다. 코드는 INT(ABS(NWS)/1000)으로 NCICE를 추출한다. 해석: NCICE=12는 12xxx 형식의 천 단위 배수로 결합한다. |
| ICE-06 / -1.0 결측·육지 값 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort25.rst line=21]` ` If numIceFields in the fort.25 file is set to 1, only the fort.225 file will be read; if numIceFields in the fort.25 file is set to 2 then both the fort.225 and fort.227 files will be read. The fort.225 (the basin scale, i.e., large scale coarse grid) and optional fort.227 (the region scale, i.e., smaller scale finer grid) files contain the actual ice percentage values (0.0 to 100.0%) and a value of -1.0 which means land or no data available. A value of -1.0% is treated as 0.0% ice in ADCIRC. ` | `[file=src/owi_ice.F line=367]` ` CV(1) = CiceB(xi,yi) `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=368]` ` CV(2) = CiceB(xi+1,yi) `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=369]` ` CV(3) = CiceB(xi+1,yi+1) `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=370]` ` CV(4) = CiceB(xi,yi+1) `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=374]` ` if(CV(k) .ge. 0.d0) then `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=375]` ` CVbar = CVbar + CV(k) `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=376]` ` ibar = ibar + 1 `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=379]` ` if (ibar.gt.0) CVbar = CVbar/real(ibar,8) `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=381]` ` if (CV(k).lt.0.d0) CV(k) = CVbar `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=385]` ` Cice1=w1*CV(1)+w2*CV(2)+w3*CV(3)+w4*CV(4) ` | 문서는 -1.0%를 0.0%로 처리한다고 적는다. 코드는 보간 셀의 음수 모서리 값을 비음수 모서리 평균으로 바꾼다. 모든 모서리가 음수일 때만 평균의 초기값 0이 남는다. |
| ICE-10 / 기본 얼음 drag 공식 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort25.rst line=7]` ` When using ice fields, the wind drag coefficient is first computed by the usual Garratt formula based on wind speed, then a wind drag coefficient is computed by a new function called IceCube that is based solely on the percent of ice coverage, then the maximum of the Garratt and IceCube drag coefficients is taken but must still be less than or equal to the drag cap in ADCIRC. If there is no ice in a particular area, then the drag coefficient is the same as Garratt. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort25.rst line=43]` ` The IceCube formulation is used if the DragLawString is not specified in the fort.15, or is specified as “IceCube” or “default”. IceCube is a cubic function that represents a smoother transition between Garratt and the RaysIce formulation for low ice cover percentages and low wind speeds. This function is as follows:” `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort25.rst line=47]` ` \mathrm{CDF} = [0.075 + 0.75 \mathrm{IC} – 0.9 \mathrm{IC}^2 + 0.2 \mathrm{IC}^3 ] 10^{-2} \text{ (IceCube formulation, this is the default)} ` | `[file=src/wind.F line=132]` ` CHARACTER(len=10) :: DragLawString = 'default' ! controls wind drag formulation `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=736]` ` SELECT CASE(TRIM(DragLawString)) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=767]` ` CASE("Lupkes","default","Powell") `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=776]` ` pic = min(PercentIce*0.01d0,1d0) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=777]` ` Cform = Cform_max*(1d0-pic)**beta `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=778]` ` WindIceDrag = WindDrag*(1d0 - pic) + (Cskin + Cform)*pic ` | 문서는 미지정 또는 default에서 IceCube를 사용한다고 적는다. 코드는 default에서 Lupkes 분기를 사용한다. 코드의 옛 주석 124–127은 실제 CASE와 다르다. |
| ICE-11 / IceCube의 IC=0 수치·IC 정의 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort25.rst line=39]` ` in which IC is the ice concentration varying from 0.0 to 1.0 for open water and complete ice cover conditions, respectively. To specify this quadratic formulation for the wind drag, set the DragLawString parameter in the fort.15 file to “RaysIce”. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort25.rst line=47]` ` \mathrm{CDF} = [0.075 + 0.75 \mathrm{IC} – 0.9 \mathrm{IC}^2 + 0.2 \mathrm{IC}^3 ] 10^{-2} \text{ (IceCube formulation, this is the default)} `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort25.rst line=49]` ` When IC = 0 the drag coefficient is equal to the minimum value for Garratt (.000075), when IC = 50 it has a value of 0.0025 and a zero gradient, and when IC = 100 it has a value of 0.00125. ` | `[file=src/wind.F line=761]` ` pic = PercentIce*0.01d0 `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=762]` ` IceDrag = (0.075d0 + 0.75d0*pic - 0.9d0*pic*pic `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=763]` ` &              + 0.2d0*pic*pic*pic)*0.01d0 ` | 문서의 IC=0 수치는 .000075이다. 실제 식의 상수항은 0.075×0.01=.00075이다. 문서는 IC 범위를 0–1로 정의하고 다음 문장에서는 IC=50·100을 사용한다. 해석: 마지막 문장의 50·100은 percent에 해당한다. |
| NA-36 / directional_effective_roughness_length: 해상 거칠기 식 | `[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst line=65]` ` .. math:: z_{0m}=\frac{0.018}{g} c_d \left \Vert \mathbf{w} \right \\| ` | `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=2950]` ` z0m=(0.018d0/G)*WindDragCo*WindMag**2.d0 ` | 문서는 풍속 크기의 1제곱을 적는다. 코드는 WindMag**2.d0를 사용한다. 원문 식은 인용 칸에 그대로 적었다. |
| F23-01 / 읽기 조건 ABS(NWS)>=100 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort23.rst line=6]` `` The fort.23 file contains wave radiation stress data that can be used to drive ADCIRC either by itself or in combination with other forcing (including winds). This file is read when ABS(:ref:`NWS <NWS>`) >= 100 in the :ref:`Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File <fort15>`. NRS=2에서는 STWAVE 제어 형식을 읽는다. NRS=3·4·5에서 이 초기화는 fort.23을 읽지 않는다. |

Set up time step information for hotstart file. `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=725]` ` dtChange = DTDPHS - DTDP `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=727]` ` IF ( (abs(dtChange).gt.1.0e-10).OR. `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=731]` ` CHOTHS=.TRUE. `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=732]` ` IF (DTDPHS.NE.DTDP) THEN `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=734]` ` RNDAYHS=TimeLoc/86400.d0 `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=735]` ` NTHS=INT((RNDAY-RNDAYHS)*(86400.d0/DTDP)+0.5d0) `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=737]` ` call logMessage(INFO,"The time step size is different "// `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=738]` ` &        "from the time step size listed in the hotstart file. "// `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=739]` ` &        "The total number of time steps in this "// `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=740]` ` &        "run has been recalculated accordingly.") `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=746]` ` call recalculateStartingAndEndingTimestepsForOutput( ` | 문서는 DTDP 변경을 금지한다. 코드는 달라진 timestep을 처리하는 분기를 갖는다. 코드는 끝 timestep과 출력 시작·끝 timestep을 다시 계산한다. 실행 가능성의 모든 조합을 검증한 것은 아니다. |

| MET-47 / NWS=±4의 prep 입력: list-directed | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=214]` ` * Each data line must have the format I8, 3E13.5. Data input lines are repeated for as many nodes as desired. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=215]` ` * A line containing the # symbol in column 2 indicates met data at the next time increment begins on the following line. ` | `[file=prep/prep.F line=3396]` ` CASE(4) `<br>`[file=prep/prep.F line=3406]` ` READ(22,'(A80)',END=9999) PBLJAGF `<br>`[file=prep/prep.F line=3407]` ` IF(PBLJAGF(2:2).EQ.'#') THEN `<br>`[file=prep/prep.F line=3416]` ` READ(PBLJAGF,*,END=9999) NHG,U,V,PR `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3400]` ` 171     READ(PBLJAGF,'(I8,5E13.5)') NHG,WVNX2(NHG),WVNY2(NHG), `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3401]` ` &                               PRN2(NHG) ` | 문서는 각 자료 행에 I8·3E13.5 형식을 의무로 적는다. 모델 reader는 고정 형식으로 읽는다. prep reader는 같은 문자열을 list-directed로 읽는다. 해석: 고정 폭과 구분자로 나눈 형식의 허용 입력이 다르다. 실행 결과는 확인하지 않음. |

## References

### Source code (this scan, 2026-05-05)

- `src/read_input.F` — main fort.15 reader (`READ_INPUT`, `READ_INPUT_3D`).
- `src/nodalattr.F` — fort.13 reader (`ReadNodalAttr`).
- `prep/read_global.F` — fort.14 mesh assembler.
- `src/wind.F`, `src/owiwind.F` — fort.22 / NWS-dispatched met readers.
- `src/rs2.F` — fort.23 wave radiation stress reader.
- `src/owi_ice.F` — fort.25 ice forcing reader.
- `src/cstart.F`, `src/hstart.F` — cold/hot start reopen logic for outputs.
- `src/write_output.F` — `initOutput2D` / `writeOutput2D` / `initOutput3D` / `writeOutput3D` writers.
- `prep/prep.F`, `prep/post.F` — `PREP80` writer / `POST*` mergers.
- `src/logging.F90` — modern logging (replaces fort.33).

Full subroutine inventory: `[file=models/adcirc/manuals/refs/subroutines.md]`.

### Manuals (curated, retrieved 2026-05-05 against `manuals` collection, model=adcirc)

- `[file=wiki:adcirc:File_formats]` — top-level "Required: every run needs fort.14 + fort.15" statement.
- `[file=wiki:adcirc:Fort.14_file]`, `[file=wiki:adcirc:Fort.14_file_format]` — mesh format, boundary conditions reference.
- `[file=wiki:adcirc:Fort.15_file]`, plus per-parameter pages: `NOLIBF`, `NOLIFA`, `NWS`, `NWP`, `NTIP`, `NOUTE`, `NOUTGE`, …
- `[file=wiki:adcirc:Fort.13_file]`, `[file=wiki:adcirc:Manning_s_n_at_sea_floor]`, `[file=wiki:adcirc:Primitive_weighting_in_continuity_equation]`.
- `[file=wiki:adcirc:Fort.23_file]`, `[file=wiki:adcirc:Fort.24_file]`, `[file=wiki:adcirc:Fort.24_file_format]`.
- `[file=wiki:adcirc:Fort.61_file]`, `[file=wiki:adcirc:Fort.91_file_format]`, `[file=wiki:adcirc:Fort.93_file_format]`.
- `[file=wiki:adcirc:Grid_Development_and_Editing]` — fort.14 construction guidance.
- `[file=pdf:adcirc:2001_Blain01 page=25]` — Software Design Description; canonical line ordering of `NOLIBF NOLIFA NOLICA NOLICAT NWP NCOR NTIP NWS NRAMP G`.
- `[file=pdf:adcirc:f13BuilderPresADCIRC2014Final_TaylorAsher]` — fort.13 builder workshop slides.
